Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! This week we’re looking at a draft from a [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20221007121403/https://magic.wizards.com/en/articles/archive/2009-06-29) PTQ Top 8 for Pro Tour *Dark Ascension*.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da888_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20120118000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo3137152)

## top 8 bracket

**K binay A**

Triple\_P

**melv.p**

SGGK

**IppoM**

GSMY100

**mrwilson**

TFujimoto

### Semi-finals

**K binay A, 2-1**

### Finals

**K binay A, 2-0**

### Champion

**K binay A, 2-1**
